# 活动剧情概要

# 星乃一歌 & Leo/need 活动剧情整理

> 来源：Sekaipedia + 官方推特 + Exa 搜索

---

## 📋 Leo/need 组合信息

- **成员**：星乃一歌（吉他&主唱）、天马咲希（键盘）、望月穗波（鼓）、日野森志步（贝斯）
- **组合名含义**：Leo = 狮子座流星雨（ Les Léonides）的法语，/ 代表四人幼时一起见到的流星，need = 大家都是必要的
- **活动形式**：青梅竹马四人组，从疏远到重归于好，组建乐队

---

## 🎵 活动剧情时间线

### 第137期活动：あの日見た夜空は、いつかの未来へ（那天看到的星空，通向未来的某一天）

**时间**：2025年7月17日 - 7月29日
**主角**：Leo/need 全员

**剧情概要**：
Leo/need 的出道曲虚拟歌手版本在网上走红。随着人气增长，女孩们决心更加努力地作为职业音乐人活动。然而，SEKAI 里又突然出现了一个新的场所……？

**卡面**：
- 「Our Journey」星乃一歌（限定）
- 「Because the Four of Us Are Together」天马咲希（限定）
- 「The Words I Couldn't Say」望月穗波（限定）
- 「When We Get on This Train」日野森志步（限定）

---

### 第195期活动：Path made by faith（信仰铺就的道路）

**时间**：2026年2月10日 - 2月26日
**主角**：Leo/need

**剧情概要**：
自从单独演唱会之后，Leo/need 的工作越来越顺利。有一天，她们获得了一个在热门音乐节目上演出的机会，女孩们非常兴奋——这是她们第一次上电视节目！然而，志步在参演乐队成员名单中发现了某个对她来说非常特别的名字……

**卡面**：
- 「Let My "Present" Self Resonate」日野森志步（永久）
- 「A Famous Producer Has Arrived!」天马咲希（永久）
- 「The Best Practice Partner」巡音流歌（永久）
- 「An Unexpected Mistake That Brings Smiles」星乃一歌（永久）
- 「Leader and Leader」望月穗波（永久）

---

### 第203期活动：あたたかな思い出を辿って（追溯温暖的记忆）

**时间**：2026年4月30日 - 5月6日
**主角**：Leo/need

**剧情概要**：
在繁忙的日程中，Leo/need 的成员们终于从事务所那里得到了一些休息时间。在讨论要去哪里、一起做什么的时候，穗波突然回忆起了小时候的某个记忆……

**卡面**：
- 「Because Everyone Was There With Me」望月穗波（限定）
- 「A Surprise Meeting!」天马咲希（限定）
- 「An Unexpected Visitor」镜音连（限定）
- 「Reading Picture Books Together」日野森志步（永久）
- 「The Yakisoba Pan Museum of My Dreams」星乃一歌（永久）

---

## 🎵 单曲信息

### 12th Single：「透明なパレット/スター」
- **发售日**：2026年5月13日
- 《透明なパレット》—— Leo/need 的歌曲，关于「变强、挺起胸膛、引导大家」
- 《スター》—— 音乐连接心意，世界因此展开

### 13th Single：「花結び/このまんまでいこう」
- **即将发售**
- 《花結び》—— 「终于察觉到的'心意'。如果它描绘的是不想要的未来，那我——」

## 🎵 新曲预告（2026年7月27日）

- **创作者**：卯花ロク（@ukaroku）
- **类型**：Leo/need 活动曲（書き下ろし = 全新创作）
- **背景**：卯花ロク 之前已经为 Leo/need 创作了形象曲「幽光、1/fのゆらめき」（收录在「セカイノオト vol.1」），这次又为活动曲创作

## Main Story Episode 2

Saki: Come on, Ichi! Let's go find Hona and Shiho!
Ichika: Saki... Actually...
Saki: Hmm? What is it?
Ichika: I-It's just that...lunch break is too short to go over for a chat! We wouldn't have time to eat our lunch.
Saki: We can talk while eating. Come on, let's go!
Ichika: Uhm... Okay, sure...
Ichika: (I should explain the situation to her...)
Ichika: (On the other hand, Saki being back might change things between Honami, Shiho, and me...)
Saki: Look, she's over there! Shiho!
Shiho: Saki?! What are you doing here?
Saki: Teehee! Surprised to see me? I've been discharged from the hospital and, from today, I'm back at school!
Shiho: Oh, really? That's good......
Saki: Huh? Shiho, is something wrong?
Honami: No way... Saki?!
Saki: Hona! It's been ages! I just came back to school from today!
Honami: I didn't know you were coming back! That's really great news.
Honami: I, um... I have to go now. Sorry.
Saki: Oh, you do?
Honami: Um...
Shiho: My next lesson's in a different classroom, so I've got to go now too.
Saki: What's the hurry? I thought we could have lunch together!
Honami: I... I already promised a friend I'd meet her for lunch. You take care, Saki.
Saki: Hona, but... Gosh, she seemed in a real rush.
Ichika: .....
Saki: What's up with those two...
Ichika: They're probably just really busy. It gets like this in high school.
Ichika: A lot of things are... different now.
Saki: .....
Saki: I guess so... Everyone wants to get the most out of their high school life.
Saki: That's what I should be doing as well! You only live once!
Saki: Thanks for coming with, Ichi!
Ichika: No problem.
Ichika: (Nothing's going to change, is it...)
Honami's Classmate A: Let's walk home together, Honami!
Honami: Okay.
Honami's Classmate B: Did you hear? The class next door got a new student today.
Honami's Classmate A: Ah, yeah, that girl with the dyed hair. Didn't you talk to her at lunch break, Honami?
Honami: Y-Yes. We used to go to the same school.
Honami's Classmate A: So you already knew her. I heard she was ill and just recovered enough to come back to school?
Honami: That's right. I didn't know she was coming back today. It was something of a shock for me to see her.
Honami's Classmate B: It was nice of her to remember you and come say hi.
Honami: Yes... She's very kind, and always cheerful.
Shiho: .....
Honami's Classmate A: Oh, are you going home now, Hinomori?
Shiho: Why, you need something?
Honami's Classmate A: Uh... No, I was just asking.
Shiho: I see. Well then, bye.
Honami: .....
Honami's Classmate B: She's kind of cold and unapproachable.
Honami's Classmate A: Yeah. I find her kind of difficult to talk to too. I heard she's in a band, so I'm guessing that thing she was carrying was a guitar?
Honami: It's a bass guitar.
Honami's Classmate A: You can tell?
Honami: I, um...heard someone mention it.
others: Apparently, she's always been like this. A girl who was in the same class as her junior high told me.|Honami's Classmate B
Honami's Classmate B: She gets hostile when anyone tries to make friends with her.
Honami: .....
Honami's Classmate A: Really? Maybe she prefers to be alone.
Honami: Hey, um...
Honami's Classmate B: What's up?
Honami: I was thinking of going to the bookstore on the way home. Want to come with me?
Honami's Classmate B: Sure, sounds good!
Honami: Great! Oh, and by the way...

## Main Story Episode 3

Saki: Let's celebrate my joining the soft tennis club with bubble tea! Cheers!
Ichika: Cheers! It's good you found a club you like.
Saki: Thanks for tagging along and checking them out with me!
Ichika: You know, you should take it easy at first. The club captain said it's a very relaxed club, but you haven't done any sports in a while.
Saki: True! The club captain and the student advisor seemed really nice, didn't they?
Saki: I was worried they'd be against me joining a sports club since I'm probably not going to be much use after being ill for such a long time.
Ichika: Saki, you shouldn't think like that...
Saki: Anyway! Becoming a member of a school club is the first step in my wonderful school life!
Saki: And the next step is...this!
Ichika: A bubble tea cafe?
Saki: Yup! I've been wanting to go for bubble tea with friends after school for so long! I missed the peak of the bubble tea craze, but never mind!
Saki: Back when everyone was talking about how awesome bubble tea was, I was subsisting on bland hospital food.
Ichika: Saki... Poor you...
Saki: Well, I'm fine now, so I'm going to gorge myself on all sorts of delicious things!
Saki: Like that apple pie we shared once. Do you remember?
Ichika: I don't think so?
Saki: You know, back when we were in elementary. You were obsessed with Miku songs and had us try playing together. Does that jog your memory?
Ichika: Ah, yes. Honami learned to play drums in the school brass band, so we all brought instruments and met up at your house.
Saki: Yes! We got so excited about starting a band! But you know how that went.
Ichika: It was all over the place.
Saki: But Shiho got so into it, she said we had to practice until we could play the song perfectly! Nothing could change her mind.
Saki: So we carried on until everyone was exhausted, and then Hona said she brought us an apple pie to share. It tasted divine.
Saki: Ahaha...
Saki: It feels a bit lonely. Just the two of us.
Ichika: ..... ...Yeah, a bit.
Saki: Bye, Ichi! See you tomorrow!
Ichika: Take care, Saki!
Ichika: (I knew Saki would want to hang out with our old group.)
Ichika: (If only I could make that happen... But it doesn't seem doable at this point...
Ichika: Huh? Did my phone just flash?
Miku ln: Hello! You look sad. Is something wrong?|Miku
Ichika: Wh-What?! Is this...a hologram? Are you... Miku?!
Miku ln: Yes, that's me. It's nice to meet you, Ichika.|Miku
Ichika: You know my name?
Miku ln: Hehe. Sorry, if I surprised you.|Miku
Miku ln: I came to say hello, and to invite you over.|Miku
Ichika: Wait, what?
Miku ln: You can bring others along, if you'd like. Your friends, for example.|Miku
Miku ln: I'll be waiting for you in SEKAI. Bye, Ichika!|Miku
Ichika: She disappeared!
Ichika: I can't get the hologram to reappear. How did it even get on my phone in the first place?
Ichika: (She looked just like the Miku in my dream. I wonder if the two are connected somehow...?)
Ichika: No way...
Ichika: But... It'd be fun to get the band together again, and have Miku singing with us.
Saki: Good morning, Shiho!
Shiho: Morning.
Saki: Do you have time after school today? Because--
Shiho: No. I'm working today.
Saki: Oh, there she goes...
Honami: Oh...
Saki: Ah! Hona--
others: Hi, Honami!|Honami's Classmate
Honami: H-Hi! Did you get your homework done this time?
Saki: Oh, she's gone too...
Saki: Why are they both like that?
Saki: It's like they're avoiding me.
Ichika: .....
Saki: Ichi... Did something happen when I was in the hospital?
Ichika: !!
Saki: Never mind! I guess that I just caught them at a bad time. I'll try again sometime later!
Saki: I was wondering, do you still listen to Miku?
Ichika: Oh, yeah. I do.
Ichika: (Saki noticed that something's not right, but she's not asking about it because she doesn't want to upset me.)
Ichika: (I feel so bad for her. She hasn't done anything wrong.)
Saki: That's cool! So, what's hot recently? Can you recommend some songs?
Ichika: Sure. Take a look at this playlist. Anything in here is pretty good.
Saki: There's a song called Untitled? What's this one about?
Ichika: Untitled? I don't remember adding that.
Ichika: !!
Saki: Eek! My eyes!
Ichika: What was that... Saki, are you all right?
Saki: Yeah, but...where are we?
Ichika: It looks like we're in a classroom...

## Main Story Episode 4

Saki: Wha-?! I don't recognize this school! How did we get here? We were in our own schools courtyard just a moment ago!
Ichika: I don't know... And it's eerily empty.
Ichika: You hear that? Cicadas. You don't usually hear them at this time of year, right?
Saki: I hear something else, too. Someone playing guitar, and... singing?
Ichika: I may be imagining it, but it sounds like Miku.
Saki: You mean, someone's playing a Miku song?
Ichika: Sounds like it. Let's try to find them.
Saki: I think the sound is coming from over there.
Honami: Oh!
Shiho: !!
Saki: Hona and Shiho?!
Shiho: Saki... Ichika... What are you two doing here?
Ichika: We're... not sure how we got here.
Shiho: I see. Sigh. I'm going to be late for work...
Honami: Um, I also suddenly ended up here somehow. Then I ran into Shiho.
Honami: We don't know what's going on, but we heard singing, and decided to follow the sound.
Saki: We were thinking the same thing! Let's go together.
Honami: The singing was coming from this classroom.
Saki: H-Hello? Is anybody here?
Ichika: (This classroom seems familiar, as if I'd been here before. Ah, that's right. I dreamt about meeting Miku here.
Miku ln: There you are at last! You sure kept me waiting.|???
Ichika: What?!
Miku ln: Welcome.|Miku
Saki: M-Miku?!
Shiho: What's this supposed to be?
Honami: Is this really Miku? No, it must be a projection, right?
Miku ln: Would you like to shake hands to see if I'm real? Here.|Miku
Ichika: I can... shake your hand?
Miku ln: It's good to finally meet you in person, Ichika.|Miku
Ichika: I can feel your hand! You're real!
Ichika: I saw you in a dream, and you looked just like this! I have so many questions!
Shiho: Me too. First of all, what is this place?
Miku ln: This is SEKAI, another world.|Miku
Ichika: Huh?
Miku ln: Welcome to your SEKAI!|Miku
Ichika: What do you mean?
Miku ln: It's a world created by your feelings.|Miku
Honami: How can that be possible?
Miku ln: SEKAI are molded by what's in people's hearts. Your feelings also weave together into songs here.|Miku
Miku ln: I'm sure some of the things you see here will seem familiar to you.|Miku
Shiho: I don't understand any of this. If this is some kind of a joke...
Shiho: Wait. What's my bass doing here? I didn't take it out of its case...
Ichika: My guitar is here too! It should be back in my room at home...
Saki: Look, there's a keyboard and drums too! It's as if someone set everything up for band practice.
Saki: Speaking of which, I'd LOVE us to play together!
Miku ln: Why not? You're welcome to use the instruments.|Miku
Ichika: What?
Miku ln: Isn't that what you wanted too, Ichika? To play as a band?|Miku
Saki: You're saying it's okay for us to play right here? That's awesome! Let's do this!
Honami: B-But... I promised a friend to meet her after school today.
Shiho: And I've got work. Besides, there's no song we'd all know well enough to play together.
Saki: There's one! That song we practiced together years ago!
Shiho: .....
Honami: Yes... I remember that one...
Saki: Then let's play it! We all know it, and it's not difficult!
Saki: Plus, it's one of Miku's songs, so it's appropriate!
Miku ln: Oh, it's something I sang? I'd like to hear you play it.|Miku
Saki: You hear that? How can you say no?
Saki: Ichi, aren't you up for it?
Ichika: Well... I...
Ichika: I want to try it. I think it'd be really nice to play that song together again.
Saki: See? Ichi wants to play too! Shiho, Hona, don't make me beg you!
Shiho: No, I'm leaving. How do I get out of this place? Miku, you must know?
Miku ln: Yes, of course. But I'm not telling you until after you've performed the song.|Miku
Shiho: What.
Miku ln: Hehe. You got me curious and now I want to hear how you play it.|Miku
Shiho: *Sigh* Well, if it's just one song.
Honami: ..... It's been a long time, so I may not be able to play very well, but I'll try.
Saki: Yay! Thank you!
Ichika: (I can't believe this is happening. We're really going to play together.)
Ichika: (And if that wasn't incredible enough, we're going to perform in front of Miku.)

Saki: Is everybody ready?
Saki: Hold on... How do we start?
Shiho: Sigh... Honami, count us in.
Honami: O-Okay. Here it goes... One, two, three, four!

Ichika: (We struggled to keep pace with each other then, but everyone was doing their best, and it was was a lot of fun.)

Shiho: I'm not interested in playing sort of good. You should know that.

Ichika: (Shiho and Honami both remembered how to play this song.

Shiho: Okay, are we done? Because I want to go back.

Honami: .....
Shiho: I don't want to be in a band with you. Period.
Ichika: Shiho...
Honami: I'm sorry but I don't think I want to do this either.
Saki: Hona... You, too...
Ichika: (I knew it seemed too easy...)
Shiho: I've had enough. Tell me how to get out of here.

Miku ln: You all played a track just before you appeared here, remember? Called Untitled.|Miku
Ichika: So it was that track...

Saki: Oh...

Saki: You're right. If I give up now, we'll lose any chance we might have at getting back together.

Shiho: I'm fine. I told you not to talk to me at school, didn't I?
Shizuku: Shii... You don't look okay to me...
Saki: Yesterday was amaaazing! It's hard to believe, but it wasn't a dream, right? Right?
Saki: Ichi, look! I have Untitled on my phone too!
Saki: Shiho and Hona must have it too, right?
Ichika: Yeah, I think so.
Saki: It's so weird that the same track appeared on our phones, but it's kind of nice. It's like we have a special connection!
Saki: Oh, do you think it's got something to do with what Miku said about us having shared feelings?
Saki: If Shiho and Hona share strong feelings with us, then doesn't that mean...
Saki: But wait a minute, they flat out rejected my offer to get the band back together, so maybe I'm wrong about this.
Saki: It's a real shame, though, because it would be so awesome to have a band. It might help us become close again too!
Ichika: .....
Saki: Ichi? Your eyes are so serious all of a sudden. What is it?
Ichika: Saki, I was just thinking...
Ichika: Why don't WE start a band? Just the two of us?
Saki: What, you want to? For real?!
Ichika: Yeah, you're right. Two people isn't really a band...
Saki: No, no, it'll be fine! I'm so happy! Thank you for this!
Saki: And thinking long-term, Hona and Shiho may decide to join us at some point when they see how much fun we're having!
Ichika: Yeah, maybe. Let's try it and see how far we can take it.
Ichika: (It may be difficult to bring us all back together.)
Ichika: (But I want to do this for Saki. If she's not giving up, I'm sticking with her.)
Ichika: (I'll do everything I can!)

## Main Story Episode 8

Saki: Band practice was fun! Luka joining us was a big surprise!

Ichika: (We were put in different classes from the second year of junior high school. I would go to their classrooms during the lunchbreak, until one day...)
Shiho: Don't talk to me anymore.
Ichika: M-Me?
Shiho: I want to eat lunch quickly so I can practice bass. Don't come over anymore.
Ichika: B-But... Shiho...
Ichika: (All of a sudden, Shiho didn't want to hang out with us. She grew more and more distant.)
Ichika: (When I asked her why, she replied that she wanted to be left alone and that was it.)
Ichika: (And then, Honami...)
Honami: Oh...
Ichika: Honami?
Ichika: Um... You bought some apple pies? Wait, is that bag ALL apple pies?
Honami: Er... Uh...
Honami: Yes... It's so embarrassing. Please don't tell anyone.
Ichika: I remember you used to get them often. So you still like them a lot.
Honami: Yes... I can't get enough.
Ichika: .....
Ichika: Saki and I went to Miku's place again today. We played Untitled, and it took us there.
Ichika: Also, we decided to start a band.
Honami: Oh, just the two of you?
Ichika: Yeah, but you're welcome to join, if you'd like?
Honami: I... I think I'll pass.
Ichika: I figured you'd say that.
Honami: Sorry... I-I was just on my way to buy something. I'd better go.
Ichika: Sure. See you around, Honami.
Honami: (Sigh)...
Honami: How much longer do I have to keep this up?
Honami: .....
Honami: I can't see any stars tonight.
Honami: (Maybe it's because everyone's not with me.)
Ichika: Honami...
Ichika: (Oh no, I forgot the fifth period is math.)
Ichika: (Maybe I can borrow Honami's textbook... Huh?)
Honami: .....
Ichika: (Why is Honami eating her lunch all alone? She normally eats with her friends.)
Ex-Classmate A: Hey, want to go to karaoke after school today? Everyone else is coming.
Ex-Classmate B: Everyone else...but not HER, right?
Ex-Classmate A: Of course not. She's no fun to have around.
Honami: .....
Ichika: Hey, Honami?
Honami: Oh, Ichika...
Ichika: I came to ask if I could borrow your math textbook... But more importantly, are you okay? Did something happen with your friends?
Honami: No, nothing of the sort! I just didn't feel like chatting while eating today, that's all!
Ichika: Are you sure? If something's wrong, you can talk to me.
Honami: It's fine, don't worry about it. It's really nothing.
Ichika: If you say so...
Honami: But...
Honami: Is it really so wrong to care about others?
Ichika: Huh?
Honami: Sorry, I was thinking out loud about something. The next class is starting soon. Here's the math textbook.
Ichika: But what were you saying...
Honami: Don't worry about it. Everything's fine.
Ichika: (After that, whenever I asked her if she was okay, she'd find some excuse to slip away.)
Ichika: (I had my suspicions...)
Ichika: .....
Ichika: If I'd only done something then, maybe things wouldn't have turned out the way they did...

## Main Story Episode 9

Saki: Ichiii! The weather's so perfect today, huh!

Saki: Heh heh heh! Not yet, but I'm going shopping after school. Wanna come with?
Ichika: Ah, so that's what you're after.
Saki: Yup! We've got a band now, so I want to have a keyboard I chose myself.
Saki: I'm prepared to spend all the pocket money I've been saving!
Ichika: Wow, you really are determined. But you know, expensive doesn't necessarily mean best. Why don't you ask the store staff for advice?
Saki: I guess I could do that. Do you see any staff around?
Saki: Oh! It's Shiho!
Shiho: Saki? And Ichika?
Ichika: Shiho...

Saki: What are you up to? Shopping?
Shiho: Yeah. You?

Shiho: Why?
Saki: Because we started a band! Miku and her friend from SEKAI are helping us practice!
Shiho: Are you kidding me? You're doing band practice with Miku?
Shiho: You can just hang out in that weird world and it doesn't bother you?
Saki: It's not weird, it's wonderful!
Saki: So, you're still playing bass, right? Are you in a band?
Shiho: I see where this is going. Listen, I've got no intention of joining your band.
Saki: Oh, don't be like that! I haven't even asked yet!
Shiho: ..... I'm not in a band, but I sometimes play bass at the live house where I work if the usual bassist can't make it.
Shiho: I'm fine on my own.
Ichika: Shiho...
Saki: But...I want to spend more time with you! Lots more! And I want you in our band!
Saki: I want Hona to join too! Let's play together, the four of us!
Shiho: (Sigh)...
Shiho: You haven't changed a bit since elementary school.
Saki: Really? How so?
Shiho: Back then you used to pester me to play with you every day. Even though I kept telling you I wanted to be left alone to play games at home.
Saki: You're making it sound like I was really pushy...
Shiho: You were, and you still are. Just like my sister. In the end, I had no choice but to give in.
Saki: Sorry... But you know, I just really wanted to play with you!
Shiho: .....
Shiho: I joined a band once, in junior high. An older member from the school pop music club invited me.
Shiho: It turned out the other members treated it more like a social club. They couldn't even play a single song half-decently. We got into an argument...
Shiho: And in the end, they kicked me out.
Saki: Shiho...
Shiho: I'm not going to join a band that doesn't take itself seriously.
Ichika: Shiho...
Shiho: Can you learn to play this song by next week?
Saki: What's this? Oh, it's sheet music?
Shiho: If you can play this with me, I'll consider joining your band.
Saki: !!
Saki: I'll learn it! I'll start practicing right away! You too, right, Ichi?
Ichika: Y-Yeah!
Shiho: Okay, I've got to go to work now.
Saki: Woohoo! Ichi, we're going to learn to play this song perfectly! We can do this!
Ichika: Yeah... Saki, thanks. Without you, I'd have given up on Shiho.
Saki: Hehe! I guess it takes someone as stubborn as me!
Saki: We can't waste this chance. We've got to start practicing and master this song!
Saki: But first, I'm buying a new keyboard!
Ichika: Right. Excuse me, could you help us with something?
Shiho: They want me to play with them...
Shiho: I thought it was best if I just kept my distance...
Shiho: Huh? Was that a shooting star?
Saki: What was that? A shooting star? Oh no, I missed it! Where did it go?|Little Saki
Honami: Saki, shooting stars disappear really quickly. You have to watch the sky carefully to see one.|Little Honami
Saki: I'm trying but I can't see any!|Little Saki
Ichika: You have to be patient. Let's keep looking.|Little Ichika
Shiho: .....
Shiho: Oh... It was just a plane.
Shiho: I guess there's no way you could see a shooting star from here.

Shiho: Because of that, my classmates started to avoid me.
Ichika: .....

Shiho: I snapped at them for saying bad things about my friends behind their backs, but that only made things worse.

Shiho: Yeah.

Ex-Classmate C: I heard you were talking crap about me after class yesterday.

Ex-Classmate C: I heard that you always tell people what they want to hear. Some people-pleaser you are.

Honami: It's just that... It's hard to not have anyone to talk to when you're hurt. I thought it would help them...
Ex-Classmate C: A friend to everybody is a friend to nobody.
Ex-Classmate C: You thought you could make everybody like you by nodding and smiling at anything they say, huh?
Honami: No, it wasn't like that...
Ichika: Honami...
Honami: Oh, Ichika...
Ichika: Are you sure you're all right?
Ichika: If you don't want to talk now, that's fine, but know that you can always count on me. That's what friends are for.

Ex-Classmate B: I saw Honami chatting to some friend in the hallway. I swear she was complaining we're bullying her here.

Ex-Classmate C: She just wants to play the victim. That friend of hers is just the same, taking her side without even bothering to find out what happened.
Ex-Classmate A: I see Honami talking to that girl all the time. Her name's Hoshino.

Honami: I'm sorry, Ichika...
Honami: Please don't talk to me at school anymore.

Honami: I already made up with my classmates, so you don't have to worry about me. Okay?
Ichika: Honami, I don't get it... Wait!
Ichika: I knew it. I shouldn't have just left you like that.

Honami: But... I'm scared, you know?
Honami: I don't want my classmates to whisper things behind my back, or laugh at me, or ignore me.
Honami: It felt so awful, and I don't want to go through that ever again!

## Main Story Episode 13

Saki: Starting today, we can start practicing together! This is gonna be so awesome!
Shiho: Where were you thinking of having our first gig?
Saki: Our first...gig?
Shiho: Don't tell me you haven't thought that far. What are we going to be rehearsing for if not a gig?
Saki: Er... Our first show can be at...the school festival! Kind of clichéd, but I dig that kind of stuff!
Shiho: Oh well, I guess that's fine for starters. We've got to take this band stuff seriously, Saki.
Saki: But we only just got back together. Can't we take it easy for now?
Shiho: No. Once I let you get your foot in the door, your lack of discipline will affect everyone.
Saki: Ugh... You're being so harsh on me!
Ichika: Hehe. It's nice to see you two going at it again.
Ichika: Oh, we forgot to tell Miku and Luka that our band got bigger.
Saki: Oh, right! They had a hand in making this happen, so we should go and thank them!
Shiho: You've been practicing with them in SEKAI?
Saki: Yeah! They have been enormously helpful!
Saki: Maybe Hona would like to come along, too?
Shiho: That's...not likely.
Ichika: .....
Shiho: She still hasn't gotten over her junior high school trauma.
Saki: What trauma? What happened to her?
Shiho: I don't know the details. Just that her classmates had been ignoring her.
Saki: What?! But she's so nice, she makes friends wherever she goes. Why would they do that to her?
Shiho: Don't know. Whenever I tried to talk to her, she brushed me off saying not to worry.
Ichika: You were still talking to her back then?
Shiho: A bit. She looked really sad, so I wanted to find out why, but she always just clammed up.
Shiho: Didn't you notice how she was depressed all the time?
Ichika: .....
Shiho: Anyway, seems she couldn't stand being excluded, so now she follows her classmates around like a dog.
Ichika: Shiho, you're being mean.
Shiho: It peeves me that she changed so much. She used to be kind to everyone, but assertive. When someone was being mean, she'd call them out on it.
Shiho: But now? It's like she doesn't have a mind of her own anymore. If her classmates told her to bark and roll over, she would.
Shiho: It makes me so mad to see her like that.
Ichika: .....
Ichika: I spoke with her recently.
Saki & Shiho: Seriously?
Saki: That's heartbreaking...
Shiho: What she's doing is turning her back on us and her other friends because she can't face up to those bullies. That's not a solution!
Ichika: You have to understand that she's scared.
Ichika: Besides, she's the gentle type. She worries about upsetting people around her.
Shiho: Yeah, right. But she didn't care about upsetting you or Saki?!
Shiho: ...Look, I'm sorry. I just can't understand why Honami's being like this.
Shiho: Although I guess I can't really talk...
Ichika: Shiho...
Saki: Hona's always been super kind.
Saki: Shiho, remember when that hat you really liked fell into the river and Honami jumped in to get it for you? Her clothes got all wet, but she didn't hesitate for a moment!
Shiho: ...Yeah.
Saki: She puts others' needs before her own.
Ichika: .....
Saki: I see now what we should do.
Saki: We should give up on getting her to join the band.
Ichika: What?!
Shiho: Saki, are you serious?
Saki: Yeah. This will be an act of kindness from me to her.
Saki: Heh, I guess I kinda sound full of myself.
Saki: But I don't want to cause her any more trouble.
Saki: I wish I could tell her that I'll always be her friend, no matter what...
Saki: But I wasn't there for her when she needed me before, so it would just sound like an empty promise.
Shiho: .....
Ichika: I'm sorry... I told you about the bullying because I thought we could still do something now. I didn't mean to make you feel bad about what happened...
Saki: No worries, I get it.
Saki: Anyway, Hona is now getting along with her classmates.
Saki: I don't think it would be right for us to throw a spanner in the works because we think our friendship should matter more to her.
Saki: We have to respect her choices.
Saki: Moving on, we should get some practice done! Let's go over to Miku's place!
Shiho: .....

Shiho: Um, yeah? It was a really long way to that hospital.
Honami: Her mom said they put her there because of a famous doctor specialising in her condition.
Ichika: I actually went to visit her many more times after that together with her mom.
Ichika: I remember one of our conversations...
Saki: Whaaat, our teacher has had a baby?! Wow, that's wonderful!
Saki: I have to write her a letter with my congratulations! Do you mind taking it to her?
Ichika: No problem, I can do that.
Saki: Thanks! Now, where did I put my stationery... Here!
Saki: ...and please take care. There, done!
Saki: You know what, I'll write to Hona and Shiho too! Letters are more personal than texts.
Saki: They seem so busy lately. They don't reply to my messages much.
Ichika: Um, yeah, they've got a lot going on. Maybe we can all visit you together soon.
Ichika: (That's...not very likely, though.)
Saki: Teehee... I'll draw cartoon pictures of them, too!
Saki: ...
Ichika: Saki? Something wrong?
Saki: ... No.
Saki: I was just thinking that...when I give you these letters, you'll go and see everyone.
Saki: I wish I could see them, too.
Ichika: ...
Saki: Haha, sorry, that sounded pitiful. It's the rain making me moody.
Saki: ...don't forget to write back! There, I finished!
Saki: Hona might write back, but I wonder if Shiho will bother. Hehe.
Saki: Okay, here are the letters. Thank you!
Ichika: No problem.
Shiho: Saki...
Honami: Poor Saki...
Ichika: She must have been much lonelier than we realized at the time.
Ichika: So I think she'd be happy if we visited her together now.

Ichika: (But I bet she would be having even more fun if Honami and Shiho were with us too.)
Ichika: (No, I mustn't be so negative.)
Ichika: (I have to focus on learning to play well, too, to prepare for when Shiho and Honami join our band.)

---

## Episode 8

Saki: Band practice was fun! Luka joining us was a big surprise!
Ichika: Yeah. Having both her and Miku really helped us get a lot more out of that practice session, though.
Saki: For sure! Gosh, I can't wait to get better and show off to Shiho and Hona!
Saki: I can picture it now! When did they learn to play so well? I wonder if they'll still let me join...
Ichika: Hehe. Your impressions of Shiho have always been spot-on.
Saki: Thanks! Shiho denies it, but it's a fact!
Saki: Let's meet up for practice again tomorrow. Bye!
Ichika: Okay. See you tomorrow!
Ichika: (Saki makes everything fun, like back in the old days.)
Ichika: (But I keep thinking that I should tell her what happened when she was away. I just don't know how.)
Ichika: Shiho, let's have lunch together. Honami can join us today as well.
Ichika: (We were put in different classes from the second year of junior high school. I would go to their classrooms during the lunchbreak, until one day...)
Shiho: Don't talk to me anymore.
Ichika: M-Me?
Shiho: I want to eat lunch quickly so I can practice bass. Don't come over anymore.
Ichika: B-But... Shiho...
Ichika: (All of a sudden, Shiho didn't want to hang out with us. She grew more and more distant.)
Ichika: (When I asked her why, she replied that she wanted to be left alone and that was it.)
Ichika: (And then, Honami...)
Honami: Oh...
Ichika: Honami?
Ichika: Um... You bought some apple pies? Wait, is that bag ALL apple pies?
Honami: Er... Uh...
Honami: Yes... It's so embarrassing. Please don't tell anyone.
Ichika: I remember you used to get them often. So you still like them a lot.
Honami: Yes... I can't get enough.
Ichika: .....
Ichika: Saki and I went to Miku's place again today. We played Untitled, and it took us there.
Ichika: Also, we decided to start a band.
Honami: Oh, just the two of you?
Ichika: Yeah, but you're welcome to join, if you'd like?
Honami: I... I think I'll pass.
Ichika: I figured you'd say that.
Honami: Sorry... I-I was just on my way to buy something. I'd better go.
Ichika: Sure. See you around, Honami.
Honami: (Sigh)...
Honami: How much longer do I have to keep this up?
Honami: .....
Honami: I can't see any stars tonight.
Honami: (Maybe it's because everyone's not with me.)
Ichika: Honami...
Ichika: (Oh no, I forgot the fifth period is math.)
Ichika: (Maybe I can borrow Honami's textbook... Huh?)
Honami: .....
Ichika: (Why is Honami eating her lunch all alone? She normally eats with her friends.)
Ex-Classmate A: Hey, want to go to karaoke after school today? Everyone else is coming.
Ex-Classmate B: Everyone else...but not HER, right?
Ex-Classmate A: Of course not. She's no fun to have around.
Honami: .....
Ichika: Hey, Honami?
Honami: Oh, Ichika...
Ichika: I came to ask if I could borrow your math textbook... But more importantly, are you okay? Did something happen with your friends?
Honami: No, nothing of the sort! I just didn't feel like chatting while eating today, that's all!
Ichika: Are you sure? If something's wrong, you can talk to me.
Honami: It's fine, don't worry about it. It's really nothing.
Ichika: If you say so...
Honami: But...
Honami: Is it really so wrong to care about others?
Ichika: Huh?
Honami: Sorry, I was thinking out loud about something. The next class is starting soon. Here's the math textbook.
Ichika: But what were you saying...
Honami: Don't worry about it. Everything's fine.
Ichika: (After that, whenever I asked her if she was okay, she'd find some excuse to slip away.)
Ichika: (I had my suspicions...)
Ichika: .....
Ichika: If I'd only done something then, maybe things wouldn't have turned out the way they did...

## Episode 9

Saki: Ichiii! The weather's so perfect today, huh!
Ichika: I see you're in a really good mood. Did something good happen?
Saki: Heh heh heh! Not yet, but I'm going shopping after school. Wanna come with?
Ichika: Ah, so that's what you're after.
Saki: Yup! We've got a band now, so I want to have a keyboard I chose myself.
Saki: I'm prepared to spend all the pocket money I've been saving!
Ichika: Wow, you really are determined. But you know, expensive doesn't necessarily mean best. Why don't you ask the store staff for advice?
Saki: I guess I could do that. Do you see any staff around?
Saki: Oh! It's Shiho!
Shiho: Saki? And Ichika?
Ichika: Shiho...
Saki: Hey, how are you? Haven't seen you since that time in SEKAI!
Saki: What are you up to? Shopping?
Shiho: Yeah. You?
Saki: Hehe! I'm also looking to buy something♪ A synthesizer!
Shiho: Why?
Saki: Because we started a band! Miku and her friend from SEKAI are helping us practice!
Shiho: Are you kidding me? You're doing band practice with Miku?
Shiho: You can just hang out in that weird world and it doesn't bother you?
Saki: It's not weird, it's wonderful!
Saki: So, you're still playing bass, right? Are you in a band?
Shiho: I see where this is going. Listen, I've got no intention of joining your band.
Saki: Oh, don't be like that! I haven't even asked yet!
Shiho: ..... I'm not in a band, but I sometimes play bass at the live house where I work if the usual bassist can't make it.
Shiho: I'm fine on my own.
Ichika: Shiho...
Saki: But...I want to spend more time with you! Lots more! And I want you in our band!
Saki: I want Hona to join too! Let's play together, the four of us!
Shiho: (Sigh)...
Shiho: You haven't changed a bit since elementary school.
Saki: Really? How so?
Shiho: Back then you used to pester me to play with you every day. Even though I kept telling you I wanted to be left alone to play games at home.
Saki: You're making it sound like I was really pushy...
Shiho: You were, and you still are. Just like my sister. In the end, I had no choice but to give in.
Saki: Sorry... But you know, I just really wanted to play with you!
Shiho: .....
Shiho: I joined a band once, in junior high. An older member from the school pop music club invited me.
Shiho: It turned out the other members treated it more like a social club. They couldn't even play a single song half-decently. We got into an argument...
Shiho: And in the end, they kicked me out.
Saki: Shiho...
Shiho: I'm not going to join a band that doesn't take itself seriously.
Ichika: Shiho...
Shiho: Can you learn to play this song by next week?
Saki: What's this? Oh, it's sheet music?
Shiho: If you can play this with me, I'll consider joining your band.
Saki: !!
Saki: I'll learn it! I'll start practicing right away! You too, right, Ichi?
Ichika: Y-Yeah!
Shiho: Okay, I've got to go to work now.
Saki: Woohoo! Ichi, we're going to learn to play this song perfectly! We can do this!
Ichika: Yeah... Saki, thanks. Without you, I'd have given up on Shiho.
Saki: Hehe! I guess it takes someone as stubborn as me!
Saki: We can't waste this chance. We've got to start practicing and master this song!
Saki: But first, I'm buying a new keyboard!
Ichika: Right. Excuse me, could you help us with something?
Shiho: They want me to play with them...
Shiho: I thought it was best if I just kept my distance...
Shiho: Huh? Was that a shooting star?
Saki: What was that? A shooting star? Oh no, I missed it! Where did it go?|Little Saki
Honami: Saki, shooting stars disappear really quickly. You have to watch the sky carefully to see one.|Little Honami
Saki: I'm trying but I can't see any!|Little Saki
Ichika: You have to be patient. Let's keep looking.|Little Ichika
Shiho: .....
Shiho: Oh... It was just a plane.
Shiho: I guess there's no way you could see a shooting star from here.

## Episode 10

Miku ln: I wonder when will Ichika and Saki come back?|Miku
Luka ln: Hehe. You're impatient to see them, aren't you?|Luka
Miku ln: Luka! I didn't notice you were there.|Miku
Luka ln: I've been watching you milling around restlessly.|Luka
Miku ln: Well, you should have said something instead of spying on me...|Miku
Luka ln: I, too, hope that they discover their true feelings soon.|Luka
Miku ln: Yeah. But I'm afraid they're not ready to face them yet.|Miku
Miku ln: Maybe here they can find the courage.|Miku
Luka ln: I hope so. Oh? I hear two pairs of footsteps running towards us.|Luka
Saki: Miku! Luka! Sorry for barging in! We have to master this song so that Shiho will join our band!
Ichika: I'm sorry if we're asking too much, but this is our chance to have Shiho join! Could you please help us practice?
Miku ln: Hehe. So your friend is considering joining your band after all?|Miku
Miku ln: You can always count on our help!|Miku
Miku ln: That phrasing was really good. Can we try it again?|Miku
Ichika: Okay. Once again from the top!
Saki: Luka, I don't know what sort of vibe this melody should have. What do you think?
Luka ln: Hmm... How about playing it this way?|Luka
Ichika: Oh, when did it get so late?
Saki: Is it late? Gosh, you're right! I was so absorbed, I didn't notice the time.
Ichika: It's always sunset in this world, so it feels as if time is standing still.
Miku ln: The scenery is also formed from your feelings. That's why it's that melancholy, lonesome time of the day here.|Miku
Ichika: Huh?
Saki: Say, isn't this song kinda difficult? You can tell Shiho chose it.
Luka ln: But don't you think she picked it because she really hopes you'll take this whole band thing seriously?|Luka
Saki: Yeah, I get that feeling too! And the more I play it, the more confident I get!
Saki: On a different note... Ichi, I came up with this tune while we were practicing. Want to hear it?
Ichika: An original tune? Sure.
Saki: Really? Yay! I'll play it now!
Saki: That's it...
Ichika: Huh, that's really neat.
Saki: You think so? Yay! You used to write song lyrics at one point, remember? Since then I've been composing little bits in my head every now and then!
Ichika: Ah, yeah. I had that phase, but I stopped writing a while back. But, I'll be happy to listen to any of your creations!
Saki: Thanks! By the way, did you notice this?
Ichika: This is a...star map?
Saki: Yeah. There's another one drawn on the blackboard, as if the class was preparing for a stargazing trip!
Saki: I'd love to go stargazing myself.
Miku ln: You can, if you go to the rooftop.|Miku
Saki: But it's still early in the evening...
Miku ln: SEKAI is a world created from your feelings. Give it a try.|Miku
Saki: Wow! Look at that! I don't think I've ever seen the night sky!
Ichika: But how is this possible? The sun was setting only a moment ago!
Luka ln: This SEKAI was born from your thoughts and feelings, so the form it takes is based on that, too.|Luka
Ichika: Our feelings, huh...
Ichika: Saki, do you remember when all four of us were stargazing together?
Saki: Yeah, I do! We climbed the jungle gym and looked out for shooting stars!
Saki: Which constellation was it over? Cancer?
Ichika: Leo. We were watching the Leonids.
Saki: Right, right. That one. Hona tried so hard to teach us, remember?
Ichika: Yeah, she's always been interested in them.
Saki: But Shiho said none of Honami's lectures stuck in her mind. Not that I can really remember which is which, either.
Ichika: Hehe. I do, though. Honami repeated the names over and over, and she actually got upset with Shiho for not remembering any of them.
Saki: Oh yeah! Shiho and I weren't prepared for Honami's angry side, so we panicked and for once paid attention to the star map. I can't believe it was so long ago!
Ichika: Well... In a few days we're meeting up with Shiho.
Saki: We have to play that song perfectly, so that she'll join us!
Ichika: Maybe one day we'll all stargaze together again.
Saki: I hope so!
Ichika: (With Saki beside me, I'm hopeful that we can all be together again, just like before.)
Ichika: (I really want to be friends with Shiho again. I'll put everything I've got into playing that song.)
Ichika: We can do this, Saki.
Saki: Shiho, we're ready!
Shiho: I don't think this needs explaining, but if you mess up even just a bit, you can forget about me joining you.
Ichika: We're aware of that.
Shiho: Good. Let's head over to the studio.
Honami: (Isn't that...Shiho? She's with Saki and Ichika?)
Honami: (I wonder where they're going. Maybe they patched things up.)
Honami: .....
Honami's Classmate A: What's the hold up, Honami?
Honami: Oh, sorry! I'm coming!
Honami: (I'm happy for them, if they really made up. It's nice that they can be friends again.)
Honami: (It's for the best...)
Honami: (But why did seeing them together make me feel so lonely?)

**穗波**：我本来是觉得对方如果有个地方 能稍微吐露一下心中的不满，心情能轻松一些。

**穗波**：（为了实现目的，她们下定决心…… 不遗余力。）

**穗波**：（而最重要的，应该是把观众放在第一位。）

### (5)心震わせるために
**志步**：——对不起我来晚了。 今天要先做一些筹备工作再练习对吧……咦……
**志步**：黑板上怎么写着密密麻麻的字……？

**志步**：……大家在干什么呢？ 不是说好要为专场做些筹备工作的吗……
**一歌**：关于这个…… 穗波说她有些想法。
**志步**：什么？
**志步**：传单的设计与制作、演出现场贩卖的CD，以及社交账号的运营—— 把这些和练习无关的事情，统统委托给其他人做？
**穗波**：嗯。
**穗波**：……之前，我们总觉得 将练习以外的事拜托别人去做，会有些不妥……
**穗波**：不过现在我们已经没有选择的余地了。
**穗波**：——因为我们实在太忙了， 所以没法在练习和筹备工作之间找到一个平衡点。
**穗波**：这是我们第一次举办专场， 我很清楚大家希望能亲自参与每一环节的准备。
**穗波**：我和大家都抱有同样的想法， 所以一直想尽最大的努力分担大家的工作。
**穗波**：就我自己来说， 要想让演出成功的话，就必须在练习上投入更多的时间， ……可是把所有的负担全都压在一个人身上也不太合适。
**志步**：……是啊。
**穗波**：在我陷入迷茫的时候，正好碰到了桐谷同学—— 是她点醒了我，让我明白了举办专场时什么才是最重要的。
**咲希**：举办专场时什么才是最重要的？

### (7)わたし達の大切なこと
**真堂**：后悔……是吗？
**真堂**：——为什么你会这么想呢？
**穗波**：……我也明白，要在激烈的竞争中取得一席之地， 固然不是一件简单的事。
**穗波**：您说年轻是我们的优势， 让我们将其作为卖点来吸引更多的观众， 我也不是不能理解。
**真堂**：那不就行了——
**穗波**：但是——
**穗波**：我们想呈现给观众的， 不是“用自己的青春去努力拼搏”。
**穗波**：而是用我们四个的演奏——震撼大家内心。
**穗波**：……我们和其他乐队，可能有些不一样。
**穗波**：大家并不是因为想在一起玩音乐才成立乐队的。 因为我们几个从小一起长大，所以组建乐队 更多的是为了今后能一直并肩走下去。

**穗波**：我们也非常高兴哦。 尤其是最后那首歌的时候，感觉自己格外兴奋……
**一歌**：我也有这种感觉。 以前每次唱到最后一首歌的时候总会觉得有些紧张， 但今天却能完全沉浸其中，真的非常兴奋。

**志步**：……嗯。 那种感觉，不是随随便便就能体验到的。
**穗波**：……这次演出，可以说是大获成功啊。
**咲希**：——嗯！ 原来举办专场那么开心啊！
**咲希**：不过筹备工作真的很辛苦啊， 今后也要拼命——
**穗波**：啊……
**一歌**：真堂先生……！
**镜音铃**：（哇哇哇……！）

**志步**：我们的专场……怎么样？

**真堂**：——确实不错。
**咲希**：…………！

**穗波**：我说的……？

**真堂**：今天的演出让我深刻意识到， 正因为你们那真实的一面，才让一切成为了可能。
**穗波**：啊……

**穗波**：虽然这些想法与心愿，并不完全是为了走向职业音乐道路， 但是……
**穗波**：我想好好珍惜——我们内心深处这份真挚的情感。
**穗波**：这样啊……
**穗波**：能给您留下这样的印象，我真的很开心。
**咲希**：啊……那个…… 那我们接下来，那个……
**真堂**：——今天我就先回去了。
**穗波**：咦……
**真堂**：不过——
**真堂**：改天我再找你们。 到时候等我的好消息，各位就先期待一下吧。
**一歌**：啊……
**咲希**：好、好的……！
**咲希**：请您多多关照……！
**一歌**：……大家听到了没。 刚才真堂先生说的那番话……
**咲希**：不会吧、不会吧，真的不会吧，难道这事真的成了～？！
**志步**：冷静点，咲希。 不过呢……
**志步**：确实能期待一下呢。 我也觉得今天的演出挺成功的。
**穗波**：是啊。但结果怎么样还不好说…… 不过既然他让我们等他的好消息，那确实挺让人激动的。

**咲希**：就真的…… 真的能成为职业乐队了。

**咲希**：啊，是伊织前辈……还有朔！

**伊织**：没错。 最后那部分真的很震撼，完全出乎了我的预料。
**伊织**：感觉你们演奏时非常尽兴…… 让大家明白了Leo/need的音乐，是如此的动人心弦。

**伊织**：后来才了解到原来高坂也听说过我们乐队， 于是我们越来聊越投机。
**伊织**：之后我们就想反正来都来了，不妨一起和你们打个招呼， 然后我们就一起过来了。

**Get over it.**：(4)プロとしての意識
**朔**：——也就是说专场的结果， 直接关系到你们是否能和唱片公司签约是吧。

**伊织**：不过还存在着一些变数…… 最好还是不要太乐观。
**伊织**：但你们已经引起了唱片公司的注意， 相信你们早晚一定能出道。

**朔**：到时候我们就是竞争对手了。

**咲希**：难道anemone也要出道了吗？！
**朔**：咦，我没和你们说过吗？
**志步**：……没有。 听说很多唱片公司都找过你们，但你们还没决定签哪家。
**朔**：没错，我们宁可不做职业乐队， 也不要和一些乱七八糟的公司签约。
**朔**：有些公司为了吸引更多的观众， 甚至提议我们改变风格去做视觉系乐队。 但这和我们的理念完全不符。
**朔**：不过后来发现有一家挺不错的。
**朔**：虽然规模不大， 但对方鼓励我们做自己想做的音乐。
**朔**：我们觉得那家公司还可以…… 于是上周就去签约了。
**咲希**：原来是这样啊……
**伊织**：那在场的各位都出道了对吧。
**志步**：不过即便我们签了约， 还是准备以独立乐队的身份出道。
**伊织**：无论是和大型唱片公司签约，还是作为独立乐队出道， 其实没有什么两样。 要做的事没有变，主要还是靠我们自己的努力。
**咲希**：靠我们自己……
**伊织**：嗯，而且和唱片公司签约之后， 我们就能更好地施展拳脚了。
**伊织**：比如说……你们这次举办专场应该挺不容易的吧？ 无论是找场地，还是卖门票等等， 所有的一切都要自己来筹备。
**一歌**：……是啊。
**穗波**：（这次多亏了大家的帮助我们才能坚持下来…… 刚开始我们自己操办一切的时候，实在是太辛苦了。）
**伊织**：但是签约之后， 这些事就可以全部交给唱片公司来处理， 我们只需要把精力全部放在音乐上就行了。
**穗波**：啊……
**伊织**：演出前，公司会帮忙找场子， 还能请到经验丰富的工作人员来打点演出的方方面面。
**伊织**：另外，在新歌完成后，他们会为曲子制作相应的MV。 同时也会在电台进行宣传推广——
**伊织**：说不定还有机会上电视呢。
**咲希**：上电视？！
**伊织**：总的来说，他们能让我们得到更多的曝光。
**伊织**：这样一来，我们的歌曲就能让更多的人听到…… 我们当时意识到这一点的时候，真的非常开心。
**穗波**：如果能有机会让更多的人听到我们的歌曲， 那确实让人非常高兴啊……！
**志步**：……是啊。 最好能让我们的歌曲拨动更多人的心弦。
**咲希**：而且，能上电视也太厉害了吧！ 那样的话，我们的歌曲就能传遍千家万户了吧！
**一歌**：不过，要上电视啊…… 感觉有点紧张呢……
**志步**：你想太早了吧？ 能不能签约都还没定下来呢。
**一歌**：只、只是说说而已啦！ 我只是在想，万一要上电视的话，我们该怎么办——
**朔**：然而，并非所有的事都会那么顺利哦。
**咲希**：咦……
**朔**：能在职业音乐的道路上大展拳脚确实是件好事。
**朔**：但一切都得“看我们的造化”。 为了让乐队生存下去， 我们不得不应对现实中的各种挑战。
**穗波**：应对现实的……
**咲希**：什、什么意思？
**伊织**：比如说——
**伊织**：到目前为止，大家都是按照自己喜欢的方式来做音乐的不是吗？ 举办演出也好，发布新歌也好，时间都是自己说了算。
**伊织**：可一旦签约以后情况就不一样了。 因为对唱片公司来说，我们是他们的合作伙伴。
**志步**：……也就是说，我们必须按照公司给我们提出的条件 来开展音乐活动，是这个意思吗？
**伊织**：说得更直白一点，就是我们必须不断考虑如何提升知名度， 让自己的乐队更有竞争力。
**伊织**：这个话题听起来让人很不舒服， 但成为职业乐队就得面对这样的现实。
**伊织**：我们为唱片公司提供能够带来巨大利润的音乐。 而唱片公司也会全力以赴，帮助我们的音乐火遍市场。
**伊织**：这就是我刚才提到的各种宣传、演出活动……
**伊织**：换句话说，签约后我们和唱片公司之间的关系， 是建立在“利益”这个核心基础上的。
**穗波**：啊……
**真堂**：而且……在竞争中处于劣势的乐队， 是没有办法和我们签约的。 毕竟我们也要考虑各方面的因素。
**穗波**：……如果没法为公司带来利益的话，会怎么样呢？
**穗波**：比如解约……
**朔**：可能会出现这种情况吧。
**朔**：……反过来想，对于那些不怎么走红的音乐人来说， 解约或许也是一种解脱。
**穗波**：咦……
**伊织**：没错，这也是职业音乐人最棘手的问题之一。 如果自己的音乐销量不佳，最终受到影响的不只是自己一个人。
**伊织**：哪怕唱片公司坚信“这支乐队能火起来！”， 并投入重金进行宣传， 但市场的反应可能还是会不及预期。
**伊织**：在这种情况下，当事人应该会感到强烈的负罪感， 对公司的同事、还有代理商的工作人员等等——感到非常抱歉。
**咲希**：啊……
**咲希**：（原来是这样啊， 要是我们签约后没法为公司带来利益的话……）
**咲希**：（我们可能会给喜欢我们音乐、 特地来找我们的真堂先生添麻烦吧。）
**伊织**：——刚才的话听上去可能有点摆老资格。但我想表达的是， 成为职业乐队，也就意味着我们的音乐会影响着别人的生活。
**伊织**：所以大家要做好心理准备。 因为有很多乐队在签约之后会因为这种事而发生冲突。
**穗波**：发生冲突……？
**朔**：说起来，之前我也听说过类似的事呢。
**朔**：主唱一直盼着能做出一些大红大紫的歌曲，来获得丰厚的利润， 但吉他手和贝斯手却对此不怎么关心， 他们只是想单纯地享受音乐而已。
**朔**：但就是因为这种观念上的差异， 让他们产生了很多矛盾，最后不欢而散。
**朔**：圈子里的人都知道， 以前这支乐队的成员们关系非常好。
**伊织**：这种事经常可以听到啊。
**伊织**：……而且我们也差点走到那一步了。
**咲希**：是啊……
**朔**：但是对于一支乐队来说， “想把音乐带给大家”的这份热情才是最重要的不是吗？ 大家成立乐队的初衷难道不就是为了这个目的吗？
**咲希**：成立乐队的……初衷？
**朔**：当然，乐队成员之间如果能和睦相处的话那是最好， 但这并不是乐队的核心所在对吧？
**朔**：我们首先要做的， “就是让更多的人听到我们的音乐”。
**咲希**：这个……说的也是。
**咲希**：（我也希望能让更多的人听到Leo/need的音乐。）
**咲希**：（但是——）
**志步**：……嗯。我们之所以决定成为职业乐队， 是因为想给更多人带来动人心弦的音乐。
**志步**：这份决心深刻而又坚定，也绝对不可能忘记…… 我们会在今后的音乐活动中继续将这份情感铭记于心。
**一歌**：是啊，一定要创作出更优秀的曲子和歌词—— 我们要努力用音乐，触动更多人的内心。
**咲希**：啊…………

**朔**：我们首先要做的， “就是让更多的人听到我们的音乐”。

**一歌**：大型LiveHouse的音响效果， 是不是比我们之前体验到的会更好一些呢？
**志步**：这和器材也有一定的关系吧……更好的音响效果， 也就意味着任何微小的失误都会暴露给观众， 所以上台前得练得非常熟练才行。
**穗波**：为了登上大型LiveHouse， 我们要加倍努力啊。
**咲希**：（……这样下去，真的好吗？）

**Get over it.**：(5)『一番』持つべき気持ちは
**一歌**：专场能成功举办真的太好了， 而且还能和伊织前辈还有朔聊了那么这么长时间，真的很开心。
**穗波**：嗯。 感觉我们总算踏上正轨了呢。
**志步**：接下来就等真堂先生的消息了。
**一歌**：不知道他什么时候会联系我们。 应该不会拖太久吧——
**一歌**：……咲希？ 你怎么不说话，是不是有什么心事啊？
**咲希**：啊……
**咲希**：唔，那个……刚才和大家聊了以后， 我觉得有点害怕了……
**一歌**：害怕？
**志步**：……为什么？

**咲希**：我在作曲的时候也是这么想的。 自己在演出时也总是想把歌曲传递到观众的心里。
**咲希**：但……这真的是最重要的吗……
**咲希**：……关于这点，我有些自己的看法。
**穗波**：你的意思是……
**咲希**：……对我来说最重要的， 应该是“和大家一起做音乐”吧……
**咲希**：但是刚才听下来，感觉大家的看法和朔一样， 都优先考虑“把音乐带给大家”不是吗？！
**咲希**：所以我就在想……我这么考虑真的合适吗？
**咲希**：以这种心态成为职业乐队真的没关系吗—— 想着想着，我就有点害怕了。
**一歌**：这样啊……
**穗波**：……但小希的心情我也能理解。
**咲希**：咦……
**一歌**：嗯。 其实大家可能都是这么想的吧。
**志步**：是啊。和咲希一样， 我们心中都怀着“想要四个人一起追求音乐”的梦想。
**志步**：当然，有时候也会感到“害怕”。
**咲希**：……真的吗？
**志步**：坦白说，我无法想象出道后的情形， 很多事情别人的经历听得再多，也不如自己亲身体验一下来得实在。
**志步**：……正如朔说的那样， “把音乐带给大家”放在最重要的位置去考虑也许是最明智的选择。
**志步**：而优先“和大家一起做音乐”这种想法或许是不对的。
**咲希**：……嗯……

**志步**：不过，我对大家充满了信心——
**咲希**：咦……

**志步**：但唯独这种害怕的感觉…… 我认为大家应该自己找到应对的方法。
**志步**：——而且，我相信大家有着克服困难、不断前进的韧性。

**穗波**：……说的没错。
**一歌**：嗯，确实会有些担心…… 毕竟这种事，不亲身经历一下是不会明白的。
**一歌**：……无论是“把音乐带给大家”， 还是“和大家在一起”都非常重要。
**一歌**：对我们来说，这两者都是不可或缺的。
**咲希**：大家……

**Stick to your faith**：(1)『目指す音楽』
**真堂**：——让大家久等了，不好意思。
**咲希**：啊，真堂先生！
**穗波**：不，反倒是我们应该和您说声谢谢， 感谢您在百忙之中抽出时间来见我们。
**志步**：……接下来是要讨论我们出道的事吧？
**真堂**：是的。为了这件事我花了不少心思…… 首先请大家一起来看一下这份资料吧。
**一歌**：这是……
**真堂**：这份资料详细汇总了我们公司出道乐队的概况， 以及目前当红艺人的推广策略。
**志步**：首支单曲的下载量……甚至还包括购买人群的年龄分布…… 这样的数据，真的可以给我们看吗？
**真堂**：确实，这些都是公司的商业机密。 请大家看过之后千万不要外传。
**咲希**：我……我知道了！
**真堂**：在看过这些资料以后， 希望和大家讨论一下乐队的包装和宣传方向。
**志步**：我们乐队的包装，和宣传方向？
**真堂**：没错。另外不好意思，突然想问一下各位， 你们“追求”的……是什么样的音乐？
**一歌**：我们追求的，音乐……
**真堂**：你们今后的目标是什么， 希望将自己创作出来的音乐，传递给哪些观众——
**真堂**：为了明确乐队的包装和宣传方向， 事先弄清楚这点真的非常重要。
**真堂**：举个例子…… 请看一下资料的第一页。
**一歌**：啊，这个人……经常在直播的时候唱未来的歌。
**真堂**：是的。这位主播在进行音乐活动时， 总是把“和粉丝一起感受音乐的快乐”这一主旨放在第一位。
**真堂**：因此，她在网上积极地进行直播， 不仅主动满足粉丝的点歌请求， 还把与粉丝的互动放在了音乐活动的核心位置。
**真堂**：从结果来看，她不仅从付费订阅和周边商品中赚到了钱， 还借助庞大的播放量和其他方式，获得了丰厚的利润。
**志步**：……这样啊。
**咲希**：啊，大家快看这一页！
**穗波**：这支乐队在学生时代 参加了一档名为“校园乐队盛典”的节目并赢得了第一名， 从那时起她们便备受瞩目，人气不断攀升。
**真堂**：她们进行音乐活动的目的， 是为了证明“自己乐队的实力”。

### (1)今後の方針
**真堂**：——各位辛苦了。
**一歌**：辛苦了。
**咲希**：辛苦了——！

**真堂**：说得没错。 我本来就打算先和你们说一下这件事。
**真堂**：你们出道曲的销量是我们预期的两倍。 这开局确实相当不错！
**真堂**：干得真漂亮啊！
**咲希**：……！
**志步**：这都要感谢咲希和一歌， 多亏你们创作了那么棒的曲子。

**一歌**：嗯……！是啊……
**真堂**：曲子本身就很优秀， 尤其是虚拟歌手版本起到了很大的助推作用。
**穗波**：啊……播放量已经达到800万了对吧……！
**一歌**：刚发布就突破了100万，本来我觉得已经很厉害了， 没想到播放量仍然在不断地在涨，真是让人又惊又怕啊……
**真堂**：哈哈哈，确实没错。
**真堂**：……这首歌的虚拟歌手版本， 对销量的增长起到了至关重要的作用。
**咲希**：是吗？！
**真堂**：没错！听了虚拟歌手版本后， 很多人都开始关注Leo/need了。 这宣传效果真的相当不错。
**一歌**：这样啊……
**真堂**：根据这个情况， 公司内也就今后的策略进行了深入的探讨。
**真堂**：所以今天，我想跟大家讨论一下这个事。
**志步**：今后的策略……？
**真堂**：是的，在正式决定之前，我们想征求一下大家的意见。 根据公司讨论下来的结果——
**真堂**：——我们希望今后发布的所有歌曲， 都能制作Leo/need和虚拟歌手两种版本。
**一歌**：咦……

**真堂**：是的。
**一歌**：…………
**穗波**：小歌……？
**真堂**：刚才我也说了，推出虚拟歌手版本会有很多好处。
**真堂**：实际上，我们已经收到了其他公司的合作邀请， 但对方表示合作的前提，是你们必须为 歌曲制作虚拟歌手演唱的版本。
**穗波**：什么，合作邀请……？
**真堂**：没错，是美味饮料公司发来的碳酸饮料商业合作邀请。
**咲希**：难、难道是…… 泡泡美味气泡水吗？！
**真堂**：没错，天马！
**志步**：对方来头不小啊。
**穗波**：不过，为什么会找上我们呢……？
**真堂**：主要是因为你们的出道曲，引起了很大的关注吧……
**真堂**：还有一个原因就是，这次对方拍的广告， 从主题和氛围上来说，和你们乐队的形象非常接近。
**一歌**：乐队的形象？
**真堂**：没错。 对方广告的主题是“这一刻，和你一起！”——
**真堂**：对方希望通过校园里的青春生活， 来表现商品清爽的口感。
**真堂**：然后发现你们乐队的形象 很符合他们的要求。
**一歌**：这一刻，和你一起……
**志步**：确实很符合我们的形象呢。
**咲希**：哇……！
**咲希**：他们的广告一直都充满了青春的气息！ 比如和朋友一起在泳池玩啊，骑着自行车兜风啊！
**咲希**：每次看他们公司的广告，我都有种心动的感觉。 那种青春生活～我也好想体验一把啊！
**穗波**：呵呵呵。小希以前也说过这样的话呢。
**穗波**：能有机会给这么有名的公司拍广告， 真是让人高兴啊……
**一歌**：…………
**真堂**：星乃，如果你有什么顾虑， 不要客气，尽管说出来吧。
**一歌**：啊……
**一歌**：……唔，这种商业合作的邀请确实挺难得的， 所以我真的很感谢对方能给我们这个机会。
**一歌**：……不过，要我们今后发行的所有歌曲， 都制作一份未来唱的版本…… 这件事，总让我有种说不出的感觉……
**咲希**：说不出的感觉……
**一歌**：嗯……
**一歌**：我也不知道该怎么形容……
**一歌**：抱歉，明知道这能带来宣传效果， 而且也是合作的必要条件。
**一歌**：但我就是觉得有些别扭……
**真堂**：——星乃。 我想分别以制作人和经纪人的身份，说说我的想法。
**一歌**：咦……
**真堂**：首先，正如我刚才提到的， 制作虚拟歌手版本的好处是显而易见的。
**真堂**：就像这次一样，你们的歌曲很有可能在社交媒体上广泛传播， 这样一来，就能吸引更多的人来听你们的歌曲—— 从而带动销量的增长。
**真堂**：这对我们来说自然是一件高兴的事， 对大家来说也应该是如此。 而且，这也不会影响到Leo/need的音乐风格。
**一歌**：…………您说的，确实没错……
**真堂**：不过……刚才那些话只是我作为制作人的看法。 接下来，我想换个角度，以经纪人的身份说说我的想法。
**真堂**：——星乃，你既然对这件事这么烦恼， 说明这当中，一定有什么让你难以释怀的理由吧。
**真堂**：无论做出什么样的决定， 在没有消除疑虑的情况下继续讨论下去，我认为都是不合适的。
**真堂**：为了今后能更好地开展活动， 我觉得你还是尽快得出一个明确的结论比较好。
**一歌**：为了今后，能更好地开展活动……
**真堂**：没错，公司确实希望你们今后发布的所有歌曲， 都能制作一份虚拟歌手的版本。
**真堂**：但即便如此， 我觉得也没必要完全遵从公司指示。
**一歌**：咦……
**真堂**：当然，作为一名职业乐手， 要是整天任性妄为的话，同样也是不可取的。
**真堂**：可是，如果这不是任性妄为， 而是经过深思熟虑后的“信念”—— 那这就值得你们去捍卫它。
**一歌**：…………

**真堂**：确实是这样。
**真堂**：不过——因为这关系到你们乐队长期的活动方针， 所以考虑问题时最好别太一根筋， 还是仔细权衡后再做决定比较好。
**一歌**：…………
**一歌**：……能给我一些时间，让我考虑一下吗？
**真堂**：只要月底前能给我回复就行。
**一歌**：……好的。 我会尽快得出结论，然后再联系您。
